So LBFGS gradient in log-softmax:
```
a = softmax(z) # sum 1
b = conv(a,a) # sum 1
M = max b
zvec = beta*(b-M); w = exp(zvec)/sum
h = corr(a,w) # h_i = sum_k w_k a_{k-i}
G_a = 4*n*h  # shape n
dot = a@G_a
G_z = a*G_a - a*dot
return F=2*n*(M+logsum/beta), G_z[1:]
```

Top-level function `build_z_from_y(y, n, pivot)`:
```
z = np.empty(n, dtype=float)
if pivot == 0:
    z[0]=0
    z[1:]=y
elif pivot == n-1:
    z[:n-1]=y
    z[-1]=0
else:
    z[:pivot]=y[:pivot]
    z[pivot]=0
    z[pivot+1:]=y[pivot:]
return z
```
No closures.

So in LSE function:
- b = conv(a,a), d = n*b, dmax=max(d).
- u=beta*(d-dmax), w=softmax(u), D=dmax + log(sum exp(u))/beta.
- f=D.
- h = pair_conv(a,w)
- dot = a@h
- Ga = 2*n*h
- Gz = Ga*a - a*(a@Ga) = 2n a (h - dot a)

Use `np.empty` and fill. For pivot general:
```
def build_z(y, n, pivot):
    z = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if pivot == 0:
        z[0] = 0.0
        z[1:] = y
    elif pivot == n-1:
        z[:n-1] = y
        z[n-1] = 0.0
    else:
        z[:pivot] = y[:pivot]
        z[pivot] = 0.0
        z[pivot+1:] = y[pivot:]
    return z
```
But if pivot=0 and y length n-1, z[1:]=y copies. Good.

```
def softmax(z):
    z = z - np.max(z)
    # clip to avoid underflow? maybe z = np.clip(z, -50, 50) after max? If max large negative? okay.
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = e.sum()
    if s <= 0 or not finite: return uniform
    return e/s
```

Implement:
```
def pair_conv(a, w):
    n = len(a); L = 2*n-1
    Mfull = L + n - 1  # 3n-2
    N = next_power_of_two(Mfull)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a[::-1], N)
    W = np.fft.rfft(w, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * W, N)[:Mfull]
    return c[n-1:n-1+n]
```
Check with small n=3: a[::-1]=[a2,a1,a0], conv with w. We previously derived d[t]=conv(w,r) slice n-1+i. Conv commutative same. Good. For i=0, c[2]=w0*r2? Let's compute convolution numpy/FFT: c[m]=∑_j r[j] w[m-j]. For r reversed. m=2: j=0 r0=a2*w2; j=1 a1*w1; j=2 a0*w0. This is h0? h0=∑_k w_k a_{k} = w0a0+w1a1+w2a2. Our c[2] has terms w2a2+w1a1+w0a0 same. Good. For i=1,m=3: j=0 a2*w3; j=1 a1*w2; j=2 a0*w1; j=3? r length3, but c full Mfull = w length5, r length3, m=3: terms j=0 w3 a2, j=1 w2 a1, j=2 w1 a0. h1= w1a0+w2a1+w3a2. Good. For i=2,m=4: w2a0+w3a1+w4a2. Good. Works.

Let's write helpers carefully.

`fast_self_conv`:
```
def fast_self_conv(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    M = 2*n - 1
    N = next_power_of_two(M)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, N)[:M]
    c = np.nan_to_num(c, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    # clip tiny negatives
    if c.size:
        c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    return c
```
Clipping negative to 0 changes sum slightly; fine. But if c has small negative due to FFT, max unaffected.

`pair_conv`:
```
def pair_conv(a,w):
    a=np.asarray(a); w=np.asarray(w)
    n=a.size; L=w.size
    if n==0 or L==0: return np.empty(n)
    if L != 2*n-1:
       # pad/truncate? Should be.
    Mfull = L + n - 1
    N = next_power_of_two(Mfull)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a[::-1], N)
    W = np.fft.rfft(w, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A*W, N)[:Mfull]
    c = nan_to_num
    return c[n-1:n-1+n]
```
For n=10920, Mfull=32758 N=32768. Good.

`exact_d`:
```
def exact_d(a):
    a=np.asarray(a); n=len(a); S=a.sum()
    if S < 1e-12: return zeros,0,inf
    c=fast_self_conv(a)
    d = (float(n)/(S*S)) * c  # scaled: n*conv/S^2, so if sum1 n*b
    dmax=float(np.max(d)) if d.size else 0.0
    score = 2.0 * dmax  # since d includes n/S^2? Wait score = 2*n*max(conv)/S^2 =2*max(d). Yes.
    return d, dmax, float(score)
```
Great. For a sum1, d=n*c, score=2*dmax.

`exact_score = exact_d(a)[2]`. In run_lbfgs, we can track dmax.

Softmax:
```
def softmax_stable(z):
    z = np.asarray(z,dtype=float)
    mz = float(np.max(z))
    if not np.isfinite(mz):
       return np.ones(z.size)/z.size
    z2 = z - mz
    # clip very negative to avoid underflow? not necessary, but to avoid all zeros except max? max=0. Use clip -745? exp(-745)=5e-324.
    z2 = np.clip(z2, -745.0, 0.0) # max 0
    e = np.exp(z2)
    s = e.sum()
    if s <= 0 or not finite: uniform
    a=e/s
    return a
```
Clipping to -745 may introduce tiny positives for very negative, could slightly affect a but negligible. It can make zeros with true 0 become 5e-324/n; okay. Use -700 to be safe.

`choose_pivot(a)`:
```
def choose_pivot(a):
    n=len(a)
    if n==1: return 0
    # choose around 50th percentile but ensure value not too small
    idx = np.argsort(a)
    for frac in (0.5,0.75,0.25,0.66,0.33,0.9,0.1):
       i=int(frac*(n-1)); val=a[idx[i]]; if val > 1e-12: return i
    return int(np.argmax(a))
```
`argsort` length 10k okay. But this inside run maybe once.

`lse_fvg`:
```
def lse_fvg(y, beta, pivot):
    n = len(y)+1
    z = build_z(y,n,pivot)
    a = softmax_stable(z)
    d, dmax, score = exact_d(a)  # exact_d normalizes but a sum1
    if not finite/dmax<=0: return 1e10, zeros
    # weights LSE of d
    u = beta * (d - dmax)
    u = np.clip(u, -500.0, 500.0)
    e = np.exp(u)
    s = e.sum()
    if s<=0 or not finite:
       w = np.zeros(d.size); w[argmax(d)]=1
    else: w=e/s
    h = pair_conv(a,w)
    h = nan_to_num
    dot = float(np.dot(a,h))
    Gz = (2.0*n) * a * (h - dot*a)
    Gz = nan_to_num, clip
    # extract without pivot
    gy = np.empty(n-1)
    ...
    return float(dmax + np.log(s)/beta), gy
```
If `s` used from clipped e; if clip all to 0? max u=0, at least max d e=1, s>=1. Good.
`f = dmax + log(s)/beta` (LSE of d). Because we used u=β(d-dmax). Good.
If beta very large, log(s)/beta small; f≈dmax.

Wait gradient of D = LSE of d. We used D = dmax + logsumexp(β(d-dmax))/β. Derivative weights w. Good. f=D. Score=2*max d. LSE f slightly > exact dmax? Actually LSE of values ≤dmax: dmax + positive? Since logsumexp(0, negative)/β >0, so D>dmax. It approaches dmax from above as beta→∞. For small beta, D may be >dmax by significant. Minimizing D with small beta may not exactly minimize max but okay.

Gradient `Gz=2n a(h-dot a)`. Let's verify derivative of D with respect to a: D = (1/β)∑ e^u log? derivative =∑ w_k ∂d_k/∂a =∑w 2n h_k =2n h. Then softmax projection. yes.

However note LSE weights `w` sum1. If beta small, D not just max; gradient correct.

One subtlety: `exact_d(a)` clips negative c to 0 and uses S. In LSE, d is clipped; derivative of clipped d? If negative regions irrelevant. okay.

```
def run_lbfgs_z(a, beta, time_budget, pivot=None, bounds=None, z_bound=Z_BOUND, verbose=True, tag=''):
    a=normalize_nonneg(a)
    if pivot is None: pivot=choose_pivot(a)
    y0=make_y_from_a(a,pivot,z_bound)
    tcall0=time.monotonic(); f0,g0=lse_fvg(y0,beta,pivot); tcall=time.monotonic()-tcall0
    if not finite f0: return exact_score(a), a
    maxfun = int(time_budget * 0.75 / max(tcall, 1e-4))
    maxfun = max(100, min(maxfun, 5000))
    # if time_budget < 30, maxfun small
    opts = {'maxfun': maxfun, 'maxiter': max(20, min(1000, maxfun//2)), 'ftol':1e-14, 'gtol':1e-8, 'maxls':50, 'disp':False}
    try:
       res = minimize(lse_fvg, y0, args=(beta,pivot), jac=True, bounds=bounds, options=opts)
    except Exception as exc:
       print(...)
       return exact_score(a), a
    best_a=a; best_sc=exact_score(a)
    if res.x is not None:
       z=build_z(res.x,...); an=softmax(z); an=normalize(...)
       sc=exact_score(an); if finite and sc<best_sc: update
    # also evaluate last? res.x only.
    return best_sc,best_a
```

```
def active_mirror_optimize(a, deadline, verbose=True, tag=''):
    a=normalize_nonneg(a)
    n=len(a)
    pivot=choose_pivot(a)
    z=log_from_a(a,pivot)
    best_a=a; best_sc=exact_score(a)
    stag=0
    beta_cycle=[10,50,200,1000,5000,20000,100000]
    beta_idx=2
    eta_list=[0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.5,1.0,2.0,5.0,10.0,20.0]
    use_natural every iteration? We can test both directions.
    it=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       d,dmax,sc=exact_d(a)
       if sc<best: update
       beta=beta_cycle[beta_idx%len]
       w=weights_lse_d(d,beta)
       h=pair_conv(a,w)
       hmax=np.max(h)
       if not finite/hmax<=0: break/stag
       # directions
       dir_mirror = h / hmax
       dot=np.dot(a,h)
       Gz = 2*n*a*(h - dot*a)
       gmax=np.max(np.abs(Gz))
       if gmax>0: dir_natural = Gz/gmax
       else dir_natural=dir_mirror
       # for each direction candidate maybe limit to 2
       best_iter_sc=sc; best_z=z; best_a_iter=a
       for dvec in (dir_mirror, dir_natural):
          for eta in eta_list:
             if time... maybe break if many
                znew = z - eta*dvec
                # center znew to max 0 for numerical
                znew = znew - float(np.max(znew))
                anew = softmax_stable(znew)
                anew = normalize_nonneg(anew)
                scnew=exact_score(anew)
                if scnew<best_iter_sc: ...
       if best_iter_sc < sc - 1e-12: z=best_z; a=best_a_iter; update best; stag=0
       else: stag++; beta_idx += 1; if stag>20: # broaden? maybe reset z to best log, change pivot?
    return best_sc,best_a
```

Pseudo:
```
def run_diversification(best_a, deadline, rng, bounds, n):
    best_score=exact_score(best_a); best_a_curr=best_a
    pivot=choose_pivot(best_a_curr)
    z_best=log_from_a(best_a_curr,pivot)
    # times: while time < deadline-20 and restart<8
    for _ in range(8):
       if time > deadline-30: break
       sigma = rng.uniform(0.03,0.25)
       noise = rng.standard_normal(n)
       zp = z_best + sigma*noise
       zp = zp - np.max(zp)
       ap = softmax_stable(zp); ap=normalize(ap)
       # Also maybe mix with uniform z uniform all 0
       t_mix = rng.uniform(0.0,0.3)
       if t_mix>0: zmix=(1-t_mix)*zp + t_mix*0; ap=softmax(zmix)
       # run beta 1000 budget half, then 5000 half
       b1_dead=time+? 
       sc, ap = run_lbfgs_z(ap, 1000, budget*0.6, bounds=bounds, verbose=False, tag='dv1')
       if sc<best: update; z_best=log_from_a(ap,pivot? choose)
       sc, ap = run_lbfgs_z(ap, 5000, remaining, ...)
       ...
```

`perturb_a`:
```
def perturb_a(a, rng, strength=None):
   a=normalize(a); n=len(a); if strength is None: strength=rng.uniform(0.05,0.25)
   mode=int(rng.integers(0,6))
   if mode==0: q=a*np.exp(strength*rng.standard_normal(n))
   elif mode==1: t=rng.uniform(0.05,0.3); q=(1-t)*a+t/n
   elif mode==2: # smooth lowpass noise
       k=int(rng.integers(3, 101)); k=min(k,n); if k%2==0 k-=1; kern=np.exp(-((np.arange(k)-k//2)**2)/(2*max(1,k//4)**2)); noise=rng.standard_normal(n); noise=np.convolve(noise,kern,'same'); noise-=(n*a?).mean? q=a*np.exp(strength*0.3*(noise- noise.mean()))
   elif mode==3: # block
   elif mode==4: # cosine perturbation
   else: q=a*(0.8+0.4*rng.random(n))
   return normalize(np.maximum(q,0))
```
No nested. Good.

```
def exact_score_eval(seq):
   a=np.asarray(seq,float).ravel()
   a=np.nan_to_num(a,0,1000,0)
   a=np.clip(a,0,1000)
   S=a.sum()
   if S<0.01: return np.inf
   c=fast_self_conv(a)
   return 2*len(a)*max(c)/S^2
```

```
def get_parent_sequence():
    try:
        raw = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
        if raw is None: return None
        p = np.asarray(raw, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        p = np.nan_to_num(p, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
        p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
        if p.size >= 8 and float(p.sum()) > 0.0:
            return p
    except Exception:
        pass
    return None
```

Let's write `lse_fvg` with careful extraction:
```
def lse_fvg(y, beta, pivot):
    n = int(len(y)) + 1
    z = build_z(y, n, pivot)
    a = softmax_stable(z)
    asum = float(a.sum())
    if asum <= 1e-300:
       a = np.ones(n)/n
    else:
       a = a / asum
    d = compute_d_sum1(a)
    if d.size == 0:
       return 1e10, np.zeros(n-1)
    dmax = float(np.max(d))
    if not np.isfinite(dmax) or dmax <= 0.0:
       return 1e10, np.zeros(n-1)
    u = beta * (d - dmax)
    u = np.clip(u, -500.0, 500.0)
    e = np.exp(u)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
       # fallback peak weight
       w = np.zeros(d.size, dtype=np.float64)
       w[int(np.argmax(d))] = 1.0
       fval = dmax
    else:
       w = e / s
       fval = dmax + float(np.log(s)) / float(beta)
    w = np.nan_to_num(w, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    ws = float(w.sum())
    if ws <= 0.0:
       w = np.zeros(d.size); w[int(np.argmax(d))]=1.0
    else:
       w = w / ws
    h = pair_conv(a,w)
    h = np.nan_to_num(h, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    dot = float(np.dot(a,h))
    Gz = (2.0 * float(n)) * a * (h - dot * a)
    Gz = np.nan_to_num(Gz, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=-1e6)
    Gz = np.clip(Gz, -1e6, 1e6)
    gy = np.empty(n-1, dtype=np.float64)
    if pivot == 0:
       gy[:] = Gz[1:]
    elif pivot == n-1:
       gy[:] = Gz[:n-1]
    else:
       gy[:pivot] = Gz[:pivot]
       gy[pivot:] = Gz[pivot+1:]
    gy = np.nan_to_num(gy, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=-1e6)
    return float(fval), gy
```

If beta is 0? We won't pass 0. If beta extremely large, beta*(d-dmax) may overflow before clip? d-dmax ~ -0.5, beta 1e308 -> -inf then clip to -500? np.clip handles inf. Multiplication may overflow to -inf with warning; okay. Use beta <=100000.

`run_lbfgs_z`: After minimize, if res.x length maybe n-1. Convert.
```
def run_lbfgs_z(...):
   a=normalize_nonneg(a)
   if time_budget <=5: return exact_score(a),a
   if pivot is None: pivot=choose_pivot(a)
   y0=make_y_from_a(a,pivot,z_bound)
   t0=time.monotonic(); f0,g0=lse_fvg(y0,float(beta),pivot); tcall=time.monotonic()-t0
   best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=exact_score(a)
   if not np.isfinite(f0): return best_sc,best_a
   # maybe if f0 LSE slightly > best? still optimize
   budget_eff = max(10.0, time_budget - 5.0)
   maxfun = int(budget_eff * 0.7 / max(tcall, 1e-3))
   maxfun = max(80, min(maxfun, 8000))
   maxiter = min(4000, max(max(30, maxfun//3), 30))
   opts={'ftol':1e-14,'gtol':1e-8,'maxfun':maxfun,'maxiter':maxiter,'maxls':50}
   try:
      res=minimize(lse_fvg, y0, args=(float(beta),pivot), jac=True, bounds=bounds, options=opts)
   except Exception as e:
      print(...); return best_sc,best_a
   if getattr(res,'x',None) is not None:
      z=build_z(res.x,...); an=softmax...; sc=exact_score(an); if sc<best_sc: update
   # Could also evaluate y0? already.
   return best_sc,best_a
```

```
def active_mirror_optimize(a, deadline, rng=None, verbose=True, tag=''):
    a=normalize_nonneg(a)
    n=len(a)
    best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=exact_score(a)
    if n==0: return best_sc,best_a
    floor = 1e-18 * max(1.0, float(np.max(a)))
    loga = np.log(np.maximum(a, floor))
    loga = loga - float(np.max(loga))
    modes = [('top',1),('top',2),('top',3),('top',5),('top',10),('top',20),('top',50),('lse',50),('lse',200),('lse',1000),('lse',5000),('lse',20000)]
    mode_idx=0
    stag=0
    etas=[0.05,0.2,0.7,2.0,5.0,10.0]
    it=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       d = compute_d_sum1(a)
       dmax = float(np.max(d))
       sc = 2.0*dmax
       if sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
           best_sc=sc; best_a=a.copy()
       stag sign? 
       mode=modes[mode_idx % len(modes)]
       if mode[0]=='top': w=weights_top(d, mode[1]) else w=weights_lse(d, mode[1])
       h=pair_conv(a,w)
       hmax=float(np.max(h))
       if not finite or hmax<=0: stag++; mode_idx+=1; continue
       dir_mirror = h / hmax
       dot=float(np.dot(a,h))
       Gz = (2.0*n)*a*(h - dot*a)
       gmax=float(np.max(np.abs(Gz)))
       if not finite or gmax<=0: dir_native=dir_mirror else dir_native=Gz/gmax
       best_iter_sc=sc; best_loga=loga.copy()? We need if accept, update loga to best ln_new. We can store best_ln.
       best_ln = None
       for dvec in (dir_mirror, dir_native):
           dvec = np.nan_to_num(dvec, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
           if not np.any(dvec): continue
           for eta in etas:
               if time.monotonic() > deadline: break
               ln_new = loga - float(eta)*dvec
               ln_new = ln_new - float(np.max(ln_new))
               ln_new = np.clip(ln_new, SOFTMIN_CLIP, 0.0)
               anew = np.exp(ln_new)
               s = float(anew.sum())
               if s<=0 or not finite: continue
               anew = anew/s
               scnew = 2.0 * float(np.max(compute_d_sum1(anew)))
               if np.isfinite(scnew) and scnew < best_iter_sc - 1e-13:
                   best_iter_sc = scnew; best_ln = ln_new.copy()
       if best_ln is not None and best_iter_sc < sc - 1e-13:
           loga = best_ln
           a = atIndex? We need a corresponding to loga. We can recompute anew from loga normalized. But we have best_ln computed from some anew; we can recompute anew = exp(best_ln)/sum. Do that.
           anew=...
           a=anew
           # update best if sc better
           if best_iter_sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
               best_sc=best_iter_sc; best_a=a.copy()
               if verbose and it%20==0: print...
           stag=0
       else:
           stag +=1
           mode_idx +=1
           if stag > 8 and rng is not None:
               # small jitter
               sig = float(rng.uniform(0.01,0.05))
               loga = loga + sig * rng.standard_normal(n)
               loga = loga - np.max(loga)
               a = np.exp(np.clip(loga, SOFTMIN_CLIP,0)); a/=a.sum()
               d=compute_d_sum1(a); sc=2*max; if sc<best... maybe
               stag=0
       it +=1
       if it % 100 ==0 and time.monotonic() > deadline: break
    return best_sc,best_a
```

Now, `perturb_a` code without nested:
```
def perturb_a(a, rng, strength=None):
   a=normalize_nonneg(a); n=len(a)
   if n==0: return a
   if strength is None: strength=float(rng.uniform(0.05,0.3))
   mode=int(rng.integers(0,7))
   if mode==0:
      q=a*np.exp(strength*rng.standard_normal(n))
   elif mode==1:
      t=float(rng.uniform(0.05,0.3)); q=(1-t)*a + t*(1.0/n)
   elif mode==2:
      k=int(rng.integers(3, max(5, min(101,n))))
      if k%2==0: k-=1
      c=k//2; sig=max(1.0,c/2.0)
      kern=np.exp(-((np.arange(k)-c)**2)/(2*sig*sig)); kern/=kern.sum()
      noise=rng.standard_normal(n); noise=np.convolve(noise,kern,mode='same')
      noise = noise - float(noise.mean())
      q=a*np.exp(strength*0.3*noise)
   elif mode==3:
      t=float(rng.uniform(0.0,0.4)); q=(1-t)*a + t*a[::-1]
   elif mode==4:
      x=np.linspace(0,1,n); q=a*(1.0 + strength*0.3*np.cos(2*np.pi*float(rng.integers(1,5))*x))
   elif mode==5:
      nb=int(rng.integers(4,21)); per=max(1,n//nb); block=1.0 + strength*0.3*(rng.random(nb)*2-1); arr=np.repeat(block,per); if arr.size<n: arr=np.concatenate([arr,np.full(n-arr.size,block[-1])]); q=a*arr[:n]
   else:
      q=a*(0.8+0.4*rng.random(n))
   return normalize_nonneg(q)
```
Could use np.convolve direct for noise length n=32768 with k up to100, O(n*k) ~3M, okay. But in diversification few times.

`exact_score_eval`:
```
def exact_score_eval(seq):
   a=np.asarray(seq,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
   a=np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
   a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
   S=float(a.sum())
   if S<0.01: return np.inf
   n=a.size; c=fast_self_conv(a); cm=float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
   return float(2.0*n*cm/(S*S))
```

But evaluate_sequence caps at 1000, no clip. Values 1000, sum 3e10, b ~1e11, bmax maybe 1e11; 2n bmax/S^2 ~? double okay (1e11*65536=6e15; S^2=1e21). Fine. But huge sum may risk overflow if length large and values 1000: sum=3e10, sum^2=9e21 <1e308; conv n*1e6=3e10? Actually max conv could be sum squares ~n*1e6=3e10; 2n*max=6e15; okay. For parent, values likely small. We can just normalize to sum1 to keep numbers small. For fallback if max>1000, scale to max 1 maybe? Since scale invariant, set `out_arr = out_arr / max(1.0, float(np.max(out_arr)))` to make max 1, sum length maybe <1000, >0.01 if any nonzero. If sum<0.01, scale up to sum0.1. Simpler: after clipping, normalize to sum1. If max after sum1 >1000 (only if sum<1/1000), scale. For internal sum1 max ~1e-4. For fallback if sum very small and one value 1000, sum1 max 1e10? Wait if parent has one value 1000 and zeros, sum=1000, normalize max=1. okay. If parent sum=1e-6 and max=1000, normalize max=1e11 >1000; but sum after confirming S>0.01? If original S=1e-6 <0.01 evaluate invalid, fallback uniform. So if S>=0.01 and max finite, normalized max = max/S <=1000/0.01=100000, could exceed. Rare. We can if normalized max>1000, scale down to max1000 (then sum maybe 0.01?). Since scale invariant if no clipping; scaling down from normalized (so it would clip) to max1000 yields same score as normalized unclipped (which equals original scale-invariant). Good.
```
out_arr = out_arr / S
if out_arr.max() >1000: out_arr *= 1000/out_arr.max(); S=out_arr.sum(); if S<0.01: uniform
```
Good.

Now, final verify with evaluate_sequence. If verified not finite due to sum<0.01 after scaling? ensure.

Now, potential issue: exact_score_eval uses FFT on arrays with values up to 1000 and length 32768; c values up to 3e10, double okay.

Now, is `scipy.optimize.minimize` available? Prompt says can use scientific libraries. yes.

Let's consider if `minimize` with `jac=True` and tuple return in some versions: It may interpret if `jac=True` and fun returns tuple, but for L-BFGS-B, it may expect fun to return value only and jac separate? Let's verify from SciPy docs: `minimize(fun, x0, args=(), method=None, jac=None, hess=None, hessp=None, bounds=None, constraints=(), tol=None, callback=None, options=None)`. "jac" can be bool, "2sequence". If bool True, fun is assumed to return a tuple of the form (f, g) where f is the original function value and g is the gradient. This is true since 0.17. So okay.

If environment older, could fail. To be robust, we can catch exception and fallback to using `fun` returning value and separate gradient via a cache? But maybe not. We can implement a wrapper that returns value only if minimize exception due to tuple? Too complex. Modern.

Another subtlety: `minimize` with `jac=True` may not accept `args` with a list? yes.

Now, testing alignment of pair_conv with LSE weights: In `lse_fvg`, weights w length 2n-1, sum1. `pair_conv(a,w)` returns h_i = sum_k w_k a_{k-i}. Good. For gradient of d_k = n*b_k, ∂d_k/∂a_i = 2n a_{k-i}. For multiple w, 2n h. Good.

Now, exact active mirror direction `dir_mirror = h/hmax` uses h = derivative/ (2n hmax). Subtract from z (log a) corresponds mirror descent on D with step scaled. Good.

Natural direction `dir_native = Gz/gmax`: Gz = ∂D/∂z. Subtracting performs quasi-Newton-like step in log-softmax. Good.

One subtle bug: In active mirror, after current a improves best, we don't reset `stag`. We only reset after accepting line search. At start of loop, `sc` computed from current a may be better than best (e.g., after jitter). We update best but don't reset stag. Fine.

```python
"""
Search for a non-negative sequence minimizing
    2*n*max(conv(a,a)) / sum(a)^2

using:
- log-softmax parameterization,
- log-sum-exp smoothed self-convolution minima,
- L-BFGS-B refinement with increasing smoothing,
- exact active-set mirror/natural-gradient polishing,
- diversified restarts,
- optional larger-length upsampling of the best shape.

All helper functions are top-level; no lambdas or nested functions are used.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize

Z_BOUND = 60.0
SOFTMIN_CLIP = -700.0


def next_power_of_two(m):
    m = int(max(1, m))
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def fast_self_conv(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    mlen = 2 * n - 1
    N = next_power_of_two(mlen)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, N)[:mlen]
    c = np.nan_to_num(c, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    if c.size:
        c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    return c


def pair_conv(a, w):
    """
    Returns h[i] = sum_k w[k] * a[k-i], where valid k-i is taken.
    This is the cross-correlation array needed for gradients of conv(a,a).
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)

    need = 2 * n - 1
    if w.size < need:
        w = np.pad(w, (0, need - w.size), mode="constant")
    elif w.size > need:
        w = w[:need]

    mlen = w.size + n - 1
    N = next_power_of_two(mlen)

    A = np.fft.rfft(a[::-1], N)
    W = np.fft.rfft(w, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * W, N)[:mlen]
    c = np.nan_to_num(c, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)

    out = c[n - 1:n - 1 + n]
    return np.nan_to_num(out, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)


def normalize_nonneg(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, None)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        a = np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64)
        return a / max(1, a.size)
    return a / s


def compute_d_sum1(a):
    """
    For a sum-normalized nonnegative sequence a, returns d = n * conv(a,a).
    The objective score is 2 * max(d).
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    c = fast_self_conv(a)
    return float(n) * c


def exact_score(a):
    a = normalize_nonneg(a)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf
    c = fast_self_conv(a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    cm = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(cm):
        return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * n * cm)


def exact_score_eval(seq):
    """
    Score for an arbitrary sequence under the same rules as evaluate_sequence,
    except bool checking. Uses FFT instead of np.convolve.
    """
    a = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 0.01:
        return np.inf
    c = fast_self_conv(a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    cm = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(cm):
        return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * n * cm / (s * s))


def softmax_stable(z):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    z = np.nan_to_num(z, nan=0.0, posinf=50.0, neginf=-SOFTMIN_CLIP)
    mz = float(np.max(z))
    if not np.isfinite(mz):
        return np.ones(z.size, dtype=np.float64) / max(1, z.size)
    z2 = z - mz
    z2 = np.clip(z2, SOFTMIN_CLIP, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(z2)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(z.size, dtype=np.float64) / max(1, z.size)
    return e / s


def choose_pivot(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(a.size)
    if n <= 1:
        return 0
    seg = a[n // 4:3 * n // 4]
    if seg.size:
        i = n // 4 + int(np.argmax(seg))
        if a[i] > 1e-12:
            return i
    c = n // 2
    if a[c] > 1e-12:
        return c
    return int(np.argmax(a))


def build_z(y, n, pivot):
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    z = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if pivot == 0:
        z[0] = 0.0
        z[1:] = y
    elif pivot == n - 1:
        z[:n - 1] = y
        z[n - 1] = 0.0
    else:
        z[:pivot] = y[:pivot]
        z[pivot] = 0.0
        z[pivot + 1:] = y[pivot:]
    return z


def make_y_from_a(a, pivot, z_bound):
    a = normalize_nonneg(a)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n <= 1:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)

    floor = 1e-300
    a_safe = np.maximum(a, floor)
    loga = np.log(a_safe) - float(np.log(a_safe[pivot]))
    loga = np.nan_to_num(loga, nan=0.0, posinf=z_bound, neginf=-z_bound)
    loga = np.clip(loga, -z_bound + 1e-6, z_bound - 1e-6)

    y = np.empty(n - 1, dtype=np.float64)
    if pivot == 0:
        y[:] = loga[1:]
    elif pivot == n - 1:
        y[:] = loga[:n - 1]
    else:
        y[:pivot] = loga[:pivot]
        y[pivot:] = loga[pivot + 1:]
    return y


def lse_fvg(y, beta, pivot):
    """
    Returns (f, grad_y) for f = LogSumExp(beta, d), where d = n*conv(a,a),
    and a = softmax(z). The L-BFGS variable y is z with one pivot omitted.
    """
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(y.size) + 1
    if n <= 1:
        return 1.0, np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)

    z = build_z(y, n, pivot)
    a = softmax_stable(z)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
    else:
        a = a / s

    d = compute_d_sum1(a)
    if d.size == 0:
        return 1.0e10, np.zeros(n - 1, dtype=np.float64)

    dmax = float(np.max(d))
    if not np.isfinite(dmax) or dmax <= 0.0:
        return 1.0e10, np.zeros(n - 1, dtype=np.float64)

    beta = float(beta)
    if beta <= 0.0:
        w = np.ones(d.size, dtype=np.float64) / d.size
        fval = float(np.mean(d))
    else:
        u = beta * (d - dmax)
        u = np.clip(u, -500.0, 500.0)
        e = np.exp(u)
        se = float(e.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(se) or se <= 0.0:
            w = np.zeros(d.size, dtype=np.float64)
            w[int(np.argmax(d))] = 1.0
            fval = dmax
        else:
            w = e / se
            fval = dmax + float(np.log(se)) / beta

    w = np.nan_to_num(w, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    ws = float(w.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(ws) or ws <= 0.0:
        w = np.zeros(d.size, dtype=np.float64)
        w[int(np.argmax(d))] = 1.0
    else:
        w = w / ws

    h = pair_conv(a, w)
    h = np.nan_to_num(h, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)

    dot = float(np.dot(a, h))
    grad_z = (2.0 * float(n)) * a * (h - dot * a)
    grad_z = np.nan_to_num(grad_z, nan=0.0, posinf=1.0e6, neginf=-1.0e6)
    grad_z = np.clip(grad_z, -1.0e6, 1.0e6)

    gy = np.empty(n - 1, dtype=np.float64)
    if pivot == 0:
        gy[:] = grad_z[1:]
    elif pivot == n - 1:
        gy[:] = grad_z[:n - 1]
    else:
        gy[:pivot] = grad_z[:pivot]
        gy[pivot:] = grad_z[pivot + 1:]

    gy = np.nan_to_num(gy, nan=0.0, posinf=1.0e6, neginf=-1.0e6)

    if not np.isfinite(fval):
        fval = 1.0e10
    return float(fval), gy


def run_lbfgs_z(a, beta, time_budget, pivot=None, bounds=None,
                z_bound=Z_BOUND, verbose=True, tag=""):
    a = normalize_nonneg(a)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n <= 1 or time_budget <= 5.0:
        return exact_score(a), a

    if pivot is None:
        pivot = choose_pivot(a)

    y0 = make_y_from_a(a, pivot, z_bound)

    if bounds is None or len(bounds) != n - 1:
        bounds = [(-z_bound, z_bound)] * (n - 1)

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    f0, g0 = lse_fvg(y0, beta, pivot)
    tcall = time.monotonic() - t0

    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = exact_score(a)

    if not np.isfinite(f0):
        return best_sc, best_a

    budget_eff = max(10.0, float(time_budget) - 5.0)
    tcall_safe = max(1.0e-3, tcall)
    maxfun = int(budget_eff * 0.70 / tcall_safe)
    maxfun = max(80, min(maxfun, 8000))
    maxiter = max(30, min(5000, maxfun // 2))

    opts = {
        "ftol": 1.0e-14,
        "gtol": 1.0e-8,
        "maxfun": maxfun,
        "maxiter": maxiter,
        "maxls": 50,
    }

    try:
        res = minimize(
            lse_fvg,
            y0,
            args=(float(beta), pivot),
            jac=True,
            bounds=bounds,
            options=opts,
        )
    except Exception as exc:
        if verbose:
            print("  [{}] L-BFGS exception: {}".format(tag, exc))
        return best_sc, best_a

    x = getattr(res, "x", None)
    if x is not None:
        x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if x.size == n - 1:
            z = build_z(x, n, pivot)
            an = softmax_stable(z)
            an = normalize_nonneg(an)
            sc = exact_score(an)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc - 1.0e-12:
                best_sc = sc
                best_a = an.copy()

    if verbose:
        print("  [{}] beta={} fun_final={} best_score={:.9f}".format(
            tag, int(beta), float(getattr(res, "fun", 0.0)), best_sc))

    return best_sc, best_a


def weights_lse(d, beta):
    d = np.asarray(d, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = d.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if beta <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
    dmax = float(np.max(d))
    if not np.isfinite(dmax) or dmax <= 0.0:
        w = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
        return w
    u = float(beta) * (d - dmax)
    u = np.clip(u, -500.0, 500.0)
    e = np.exp(u)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        w = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        w[int(np.argmax(d))] = 1.0
        return w
    return e / s


def weights_top(d, k):
    d = np.asarray(d, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = d.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    k = int(max(1, min(k, n)))
    if k == n:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
    idx = np.argpartition(d, -k)[-k:]
    w = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    w[idx] = 1.0 / float(k)
    return w


def active_mirror_optimize(a, deadline, rng=None, verbose=True, tag=""):
    a = normalize_nonneg(a)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n <= 1:
        return exact_score(a), a

    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = exact_score(a)

    floor = 1.0e-18 * max(1.0, float(np.max(a)))
    loga = np.log(np.maximum(a, floor))
    loga = loga - float(np.max(loga))

    modes = [
        ("top", 1),
        ("top", 2),
        ("top", 3),
        ("top", 5),
        ("top", 8),
        ("top", 12),
        ("top", 20),
        ("top", 40),
        ("lse", 50.0),
        ("lse", 200.0),
        ("lse", 1000.0),
        ("lse", 5000.0),
        ("lse", 20000.0),
    ]

    etas = [0.05, 0.2, 0.7, 2.0, 5.0, 10.0]
    mode_idx = 0
    stag = 0
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline and it < 200000:
        d = compute_d_sum1(a)
        if d.size == 0:
            break
        dmax = float(np.max(d))
        sc = 2.0 * dmax

        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc - 1.0e-12:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = a.copy()

        mode = modes[mode_idx % len(modes)]
        if mode[0] == "top":
            w = weights_top(d, int(mode[1]))
        else:
            w = weights_lse(d, float(mode[1]))

        h = pair_conv(a, w)
        hmax = float(np.max(h))
        if not np.isfinite(hmax) or hmax <= 0.0:
            stag += 1
            mode_idx += 1
            it += 1
            continue

        dir_mirror = h / hmax
        dot = float(np.dot(a, h))
        grad_z = (2.0 * float(n)) * a * (h - dot * a)
        gmax = float(np.max(np.abs(grad_z)))
        if np.isfinite(gmax) and gmax > 0.0:
            dir_nat = grad_z / gmax
        else:
            dir_nat = dir_mirror

        best_anew = None
        best_iter_sc = sc

        for dvec in (dir_mirror, dir_nat):
            dvec = np.nan_to_num(dvec, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
            if not np.any(dvec):
                continue
            for eta in etas:
                if time.monotonic() > deadline:
                    break
                ln_new = loga - float(eta) * dvec
                ln_new = ln_new - float(np.max(ln_new))
                ln_new = np.clip(ln_new, SOFTMIN_CLIP, 0.0)
                anew = np.exp(ln_new)
                snew = float(anew.sum())
                if not np.isfinite(snew) or snew <= 0.0:
                    continue
                anew = anew / snew
                dnew = compute_d_sum1(anew)
                scnew = 2.0 * float(np.max(dnew))
                if np.isfinite(scnew) and scnew < best_iter_sc - 1.0e-13:
                    best_iter_sc = scnew
                    best_anew = anew.copy()

        if best_anew is not None and best_iter_sc < sc - 1.0e-13:
            a = best_anew
            floor = 1.0e-18 * max(1.0, float(np.max(a)))
            loga = np.log(np.maximum(a, floor))
            loga = loga - float(np.max(loga))

            if best_iter_sc < best_sc - 1.0e-12:
                best_sc = best_iter_sc
                best_a = a.copy()
                if verbose and it % 30 == 0:
                    print("  [{}] active it={} score={:.9f}".format(tag, it, best_sc))
            stag = 0
        else:
            stag += 1
            mode_idx += 1

            if stag >= 10 and rng is not None:
                sig = float(rng.uniform(0.005, 0.05))
                loga = loga + sig * rng.standard_normal(n)
                loga = loga - float(np.max(loga))
                a = np.exp(np.clip(loga, SOFTMIN_CLIP, 0.0))
                s = float(a.sum())
                if np.isfinite(s) and s > 0.0:
                    a = a / s
                    d = compute_d_sum1(a)
                    sc2 = 2.0 * float(np.max(d))
                    if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_sc:
                        best_sc = sc2
                        best_a = a.copy()
                stag = 0

        it += 1

    return float(best_sc), best_a


def perturb_a(a, rng, strength=None):
    a = normalize_nonneg(a)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n <= 1:
        return a
    if strength is None:
        strength = float(rng.uniform(0.04, 0.22))

    mode = int(rng.integers(0, 7))

    if mode == 0:
        q = a * np.exp(strength * rng.standard_normal(n))
    elif mode == 1:
        t = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.35))
        q = (1.0 - t) * a + t * (1.0 / float(n))
    elif mode == 2:
        hi = max(5, min(101, n))
        k = int(rng.integers(3, hi + 1))
        k = min(k, n)
        if k % 2 == 0:
            k -= 1
        if k < 3:
            k = 3
        c = k // 2
        sig = max(1.0, c / 2.0)
        kern = np.exp(-((np.arange(k) - c) ** 2) / (2.0 * sig * sig))
        kern = kern / max(1.0e-30, float(kern.sum()))
        noise = rng.standard_normal(n)
        noise = np.convolve(noise, kern, mode="same")
        noise = noise - float(noise.mean())
        q = a * np.exp(strength * 0.35 * noise)
    elif mode == 3:
        t = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 0.45))
        q = (1.0 - t) * a + t * a[::-1]
    elif mode == 4:
        x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
        f = float(rng.integers(1, 6))
        q = a * (1.0 + strength * 0.25 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * f * x))
    elif mode == 5:
        nb = int(rng.integers(4, 21))
        per = max(1, n // nb)
        block = 1.0 + strength * 0.35 * (rng.random(nb) * 2.0 - 1.0)
        arr = np.repeat(block, per)
        if arr.size < n:
            arr = np.concatenate([arr, np.full(n - arr.size, block[-1], dtype=np.float64)])
        q = a * arr[:n]
    else:
        q = a * (0.85 + 0.30 * rng.random(n))

    return normalize_nonneg(q)


def resample_sequence(a, n):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, None)
    m = int(a.size)
    if m == 0:
        return np.ones(int(n), dtype=np.float64)
    if m == int(n):
        return a.copy()
    if m == 1:
        return np.full(int(n), float(a[0]), dtype=np.float64)
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, int(n))
    return np.interp(x_new, x_old, a).astype(np.float64)


def add_init(inits, n, cap, arr):
    if len(inits) >= cap:
        return
    arr = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size != n:
        arr = resample_sequence(arr, n)
    arr = np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, None)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        arr = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(arr.sum())
    inits.append(arr / s)


def generate_inits(n, rng, parent=None, cap=40):
    inits = []
    n = int(n)
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)

    if parent is not None:
        add_init(inits, n, cap, parent)
        add_init(inits, n, cap, parent[::-1])

    add_init(inits, n, cap, np.ones(n))

    for alpha in (0.3, 0.6, 1.0, 1.5, 2.0):
        add_init(inits, n, cap, 1.0 - alpha * (2.0 * x - 1.0) ** 2)

    for alpha in (0.3, 0.6, 1.0, 1.5):
        add_init(inits, n, cap, 1.0 + alpha * (2.0 * x - 1.0) ** 2)

    for f in (1, 2, 3, 4, 5):
        for alpha in (0.3, 0.7, 1.2, 1.8):
            add_init(inits, n, cap, 1.0 + alpha * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * f * x))

    for f in (1, 2, 3, 4):
        for alpha in (0.5, 1.0, 1.5):
            add_init(inits, n, cap, 0.5 + alpha * np.abs(np.sin(np.pi * f * x)))

    for rate in (1.0, 2.0, 3.0, 5.0, 8.0):
        add_init(inits, n, cap, np.exp(-rate * x) + np.exp(-rate * (1.0 - x)) + 0.01)
        add_init(inits, n, cap, np.exp(-rate * np.abs(x - 0.5)) + 0.01)

    for width in (0.05, 0.08, 0.12, 0.18):
        for ctr in (0.20, 0.30, 0.40):
            add_init(
                inits,
                n,
                cap,
                np.exp(-(((x - ctr) / width) ** 2)) +
                np.exp(-(((x - (1.0 - ctr)) / width) ** 2)) + 0.01
            )

    for p in (0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 3.0):
        add_init(inits, n, cap, (1.0 - np.abs(2.0 * x - 1.0)) ** p + 0.01)

    Nf = next_power_of_two(n)
    try:
        freqs = np.fft.rfftfreq(Nf) * Nf
        for _ in range(8):
            if len(inits) >= cap:
                break
            power = 1.0 / ((np.abs(freqs) + 1.0) ** float(rng.uniform(1.5, 4.0)))
            power[0:2] = 0.0
            phase = rng.uniform(-np.pi, np.pi, size=len(power))
            spec = power * np.exp(1j * phase)
            spec[0:2] = 0.0
            noise = np.fft.irfft(spec, Nf)[:n]
            noise = noise - float(noise.mean())
            add_init(inits, n, cap, 1.0 + float(rng.uniform(0.35, 1.2)) * noise)
    except Exception:
        pass

    for _ in range(3):
        if len(inits) >= cap:
            break
        add_init(inits, n, cap, 0.1 + 0.9 * rng.random(n))

    if not inits:
        inits.append(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n))

    return inits


def get_parent_sequence():
    try:
        raw = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
        if raw is None:
            return None
        p = np.asarray(raw, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        p = np.nan_to_num(p, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
        p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
        if p.size >= 8 and float(p.sum()) > 0.0:
            return p
    except Exception:
        pass
    return None


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400):
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    total = float(budget_s)
    deadline = t_start + total - 25.0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    # Choose a practical length. Larger is usually better, but adaptive fallback
    # protects against slow FFT environments.
    n_main = 32768
    use_large = True
    try:
        n_test = 32768
        a_test = np.ones(n_test, dtype=np.float64) / float(n_test)
        pivot_test = n_test // 2
        y_test = np.zeros(n_test - 1, dtype=np.float64)
        tb0 = time.monotonic()
        lse_fvg(y_test, 100.0, pivot_test)
        bench_t = time.monotonic() - tb0
        if bench_t > 0.50:
            use_large = False
    except Exception:
        use_large = False

    if not use_large:
        n_main = 16384

    # Read parent if available.
    parent = get_parent_sequence()
    fallback_seq = None
    if parent is not None:
        fallback_seq = np.nan_to_num(parent, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
        fallback_seq = np.clip(fallback_seq, 0.0, 1000.0)

    if parent is not None:
        a0 = normalize_nonneg(resample_sequence(parent, n_main))
    else:
        inits = generate_inits(n_main, rng, None, cap=45)
        best_init = None
        best_init_sc = np.inf
        for init in inits:
            sc = exact_score(init)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_init_sc:
                best_init_sc = sc
                best_init = init
        a0 = best_init if best_init is not None else np.ones(n_main, dtype=np.float64) / float(n_main)
        print("No parent available. Best init score: {:.9f}".format(best_init_sc))

    best_a = a0.copy()
    best_sc = exact_score(best_a)
    print("Initial: n={} score={:.9f}".format(n_main, best_sc))

    bounds = [(-Z_BOUND, Z_BOUND)] * max(1, n_main - 1)

    # L-BFGS phase schedule. Fractions are end-of-phase fractions of total budget.
    phases = [
        (50.0, 0.0625),
        (200.0, 0.1875),
        (1000.0, 0.3542),
        (5000.0, 0.5208),
        (20000.0, 0.6667),
        (100000.0, 0.7917),
    ]

    for i, (beta, frac_end) in enumerate(phases):
        frac_start = phases[i - 1][1] if i > 0 else 0.0
        phase_end = t_start + frac_end * total
        orig_budget = (frac_end - frac_start) * total
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now >= phase_end - 5.0:
            continue
        budget = min(phase_end - now, orig_budget)
        if budget < 20.0:
            continue

        sc, a_new = run_lbfgs_z(
            best_a,
            beta,
            budget,
            bounds=bounds,
            verbose=True,
            tag="lbfgs",
        )
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc - 1.0e-12:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = a_new.copy()
            print("After beta={}: {:.9f}".format(int(beta), best_sc))

    # Exact / active-set mirror polish.
    active_end = t_start + 0.9167 * total
    if time.monotonic() < active_end - 10.0:
        sc, a_new = active_mirror_optimize(
            best_a,
            active_end,
            rng=rng,
            verbose=True,
            tag="active",
        )
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc - 1.0e-12:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = a_new.copy()
            print("After active polish: {:.9f}".format(best_sc))

    # Diversified restarts.
    div_end = t_start + 0.9813 * total
    if time.monotonic() < div_end - 30.0:
        for r in range(6):
            now = time.monotonic()
            if now > div_end - 35.0:
                break
            rem = div_end - now
            if rem < 60.0:
                break

            p = perturb_a(best_a, rng, strength=float(rng.uniform(0.02, 0.14)))
            b1 = min(55.0, rem * 0.40)
            sc, p1 = run_lbfgs_z(p, 1000.0, b1, bounds=bounds, verbose=False, tag="div1")
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc - 1.0e-12:
                best_sc = sc
                best_a = p1.copy()
                print("Div restart {}: {:.9f}".format(r, best_sc))

            now = time.monotonic()
            if now > div_end - 35.0:
                break
            rem2 = div_end - now
            b2 = min(55.0, rem2 * 0.45)
            if b2 >= 20.0:
                sc, p2 = run_lbfgs_z(p1, 5000.0, b2, bounds=bounds, verbose=False, tag="div2")
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc - 1.0e-12:
                    best_sc = sc
                    best_a = p2.copy()
                    print("Div restart2 {}: {:.9f}".format(r, best_sc))

    # Final small exact polish.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 15.0:
        sc, a_new = active_mirror_optimize(
            best_a,
            deadline - 10.0,
            rng=rng,
            verbose=True,
            tag="final",
        )
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc - 1.0e-12:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = a_new.copy()
            print("After final active polish: {:.9f}".format(best_sc))

    # Optional upsampling for better discretization, if we did not already use 32768.
    if n_main < 32768 and best_a is not None:
        a_up = normalize_nonneg(resample_sequence(best_a, 32768))
        sc_up = exact_score_eval(a_up)
        if np.isfinite(sc_up) and sc_up < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc_up
            best_a = a_up
            print("Up-sampled to 32768: {:.9f}".format(best_sc))

    # Choose among the optimized sequence and the supplied parent sequence.
    candidates = []
    if best_a is not None:
        candidates.append(best_a)
    if fallback_seq is not None:
        candidates.append(fallback_seq)

    final_seq = None
    final_ev = np.inf
    for c in candidates:
        if c is None:
            continue
        ev = exact_score_eval(c)
        if np.isfinite(ev) and ev < final_ev:
            final_ev = ev
            final_seq = c

    if final_seq is None:
        final_seq = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64) / 1024.0

    out_arr = np.asarray(final_seq, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    out_arr = np.nan_to_num(out_arr, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    out_arr = np.clip(out_arr, 0.0, 1000.0)

    s = float(out_arr.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 0.01:
        out_arr = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64) / 1024.0
        s = float(out_arr.sum())

    # Scale down if needed to avoid evaluator clipping; scale-invariant.
    mx = float(np.max(out_arr))
    if not np.isfinite(mx) or mx <= 0.0:
        out_arr = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64) / 1024.0
    elif mx > 1000.0:
        out_arr = out_arr * (1000.0 / mx)
        s2 = float(out_arr.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(s2) or s2 < 0.01:
            out_arr = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64) / 1024.0

    out = [float(v) for v in out_arr]

    try:
        verified = float(evaluate_sequence(out))
    except Exception as exc:
        verified = exact_score_eval(out)
        print("Verification exception: {}".format(exc))

    print("DONE: internal={:.9f} verified={:.9f} n={} time={:.2f}s".format(
        best_sc, verified, len(out), time.monotonic() - t_start
    ))

    return out
```
